```text
generation-resolved `180/182`
→ ordinary `70/72`
→ week-resolved `600/614`.
```

The reason remains mechanistic. `180/182` makes the local four-point cell visible; `70/72` confirms that the same incidence survives without expansion; `600/614` applies the fixed `110` once per completed seven-generation week rather than once per generation.

The author-invoked August 16 Final and full pressure-test baseline remain the inherited control for unchanged material. Two bounded August 17 amendments added the reversed external `14+13` / internal `13+14` fingerprint and the Matthean Levi-branch sliding double-`14` Mosaic lock.

The August 18 reopening is broader than a pointer-only or arithmetic-only amendment. It integrates four linked discoveries into the file's central argument:

1. Matthew's undoubled `41`-figure inventory is structurally `14|13|14`, so Matthew itself contains both directional `27`-row forms: `14+13` and `13+14`.
2. Jeconiah's exile overlap converts the central `13` to `14`, yielding the declared `14|14|14=42`, while the forwarded Jeconiah row reproduces Kenan's `70+840=910` anatomy and performs a count-switch analogous to second Cainan.
3. The January 1, AD 1 covenant datum discloses the `6|7|8` Creation sequence and crossed Abrahamic Mirror fields of `81×70=5670` and `81×72=5832`, each equal to three `27`-generation volumes.
4. The `+110` rail exchanges the omitted `70/72` slot for a Mosaic residual of `40/38`, preserving or translating the exact centers `1406/1446/1448/1486 BC`.

Because the revision changes the thesis hierarchy, §1 count logic, the §3.4 theorem sequence, synthesis, conclusion, guards, and arithmetic appendices, it was treated as a High-risk reopening. The High-risk split Unified Revision Pass, independent Pass 2 Verification, author-invoked Finalization, and full post-final pressure test are complete. Control registration, reciprocal-source refresh, archive work, and publication regeneration remain separate bounded downstream stages.

The August 20 reopening began from the now-Final File_09 cumulative substrate. Pass 1A established the structural receiving layer:

1. the proof-burden handoff from Final File_09;
2. the source / derived / generated cumulative node classes;
3. the `26/27` count-versus-duration firewall;
4. the actual/completed Enoch `5879/5880` clutch;
5. the `8132/8622` same-side complement namespace;
6. the coordinated File_09/File_69 publication deferral.

Pass 1B then integrated the exact arithmetic and argument in §5A and Appendices A.7/B.7. No inherited File_69 theorem is corrected or replaced.

The August 21 reopening is a separate High-risk amendment. It adds the Genesis *toledot–shemot* literary spine and an independently executed regular-MT Atonement proof. Pass 1A fixed structure, terminology, states, node classes, proof-burden routing, claim ceilings, and Machine Guards. Pass 1B executed the unshifted Terah-to-Entry accordion, Isaac-center matrices, Cainan cross-modal seal, Creation-to-Jacob rectangle, Jacob–Enoch `1260` bridge, and secondary Enoch–Isaac arithmetic in §5B and Appendices A.8/B.8. Independent Pass 2 and the combined pressure test verified the integrated module, and renewed Finalization was invoked August 21 2026. No inherited Enoch-centered theorem is corrected or replaced.

## 0. File-function and controls

### 0.1 File-function

`File_69` tests whether Luke and Matthew apply the same Enochic accordion principles established in `File_68`.

Its controlling thesis is not that Creation is unimportant. Rather:

> **Enoch is the operating hinge.** Creation is the outer bookend, the Flood and Shem are cumulative interfaces, Matthew's `14|13|14` is the local textual microcosm, Moses is the historical center interface, and Christ is the terminal completion. Luke moves backward toward Enoch; Matthew moves forward toward Enoch; the Book of Jubilees supplies a pro-Enochic Creation coordinate; and the Enochic schematic supplies the mainframe that locks the systems to MT regular and cumulative chronology.

The file has twenty-seven local functions:

1. preserve File_68's fixed `70/72` carriers, exact `110` rail, common `AD 65` terminal, and three-resolution distinction;
2. distinguish Luke's `76` corresponding-boundary state from the full-outer `77` state used here;
3. distinguish Matthew's undoubled `14|13|14=41` named-figure state from the Jeconiah-overlapped `14|14|14=42` occupied-slot state;
4. preserve the reduced `21+41=62` composite as a comparison state while retaining `21+42=63` as the leading full-outer Matthean composite;
5. derive the Luke / Matthew / Enochic tables first at `180/182`, then verify the same incidence at `70/72`, and only then group the lattice into `600/614` weekly units;
6. define the outcome as a discrete self-similar mechanism rather than a collection of independent date coincidences;
7. show how the Lukan and Matthean expansions complete the MT regular / cumulative Creation and Flood interfaces left outside File_68's primary Flood-to-Joshua corridor;
8. expose the external Luke–Matthew–Enochic `14+13` spacing as the reverse of the retained/native-Cainan Adam-to-Moses `13+14` row architecture already controlled in the Pentateuchal chronology;
9. show that Matthew's own `14|13|14` contains both directional `27`-row fingerprints locally and that Jeconiah's overlap supplies the one-slot completion to `42`;
10. show that the forwarded Jeconiah row reproduces first Kenan's `70+840=910` anatomy while Jeconiah's single-versus-overlapped handling performs a count-switch analogous to second Cainan's expunction-versus-retention;
11. show that the covenant datum reveals the `6|7|8` Creation sequence and three crossed `27`-generation Mirror fields under each ordinary carrier;
12. show that Matthew's Abraham bracket begins the final seven-row Levi branch and that the `+110` rail preserves or translates the Mosaic centers `1406/1446/1448/1486 BC` without changing the historical anchor hierarchy.
13. import Final File_09 as the immediate cumulative-substrate parent without retrojecting its generated companions into File_18 or into File_69's coordinate generator;
14. distinguish source-controlled cumulative cells, Final-File_09 derived companions, reopened-File_69 complement outputs, and independently controlled shared coordinates;
15. show structurally how the cumulative `70/72`, `180/182`, `490/504`, and `600/614` hierarchy underlies the already generated File_69 resolution states;
16. distinguish the `26/27/28` attribution counts from the `26/27×483/490` carrier-equivalent durations;
17. register the Shelah `6×483` / Flood `7×490` duration seam as the metric counterpart to the genealogical `13/14` grammar;
18. distinguish the actual `8132–8125 BC` Enoch-close cell from the completed `8131–8124 BC` companion and from the two figurative-Adam companion cells;
19. define the `8132/8622` operation as same-side complement geometry, not formal Mirror, and execute its cross-schematic arithmetic only after the inherited coordinate families have been generated.
20. identify Genesis 2:4 as the first-*toledot* literary hinge after the completed `6+1` Creation frame and before the recapitulated Day-6 human-formation narrative, without assigning the heading itself a chronological date;
21. map the ten major Genesis *toledot* sections, including the doubled Esau formula, toward the Genesis 46 / Exodus 1 *shemot* threshold;
22. distinguish the sons-of-Noah heading from Shem's individual heading and treat Noah/Shem death labels as derived chronology rather than explicit *toledot* wording;
23. define the Shem–Terah overlapping-boundary seam, derive the textual `+60 Terah` operator from `205−75−70=60`, and execute the unshifted regular biographies;
24. define `d=10=70÷7` as a local resolution unit only and execute the Terah-to-*shemot* `6/7→13/14→27/28→41/42` accordion;
25. distinguish literary headings, source biographical ages, variant-generated regular dates, shared-label overlays, and the `1876 BC` *shemot* threshold as separate node classes;
26. make the regular-MT Terah-to-Jacob corridor the primary new proof burden, while routing regular Enoch and Shem–Isaac to secondary body summary and Appendix A.8/B.8;
27. quarantine the whole-system `−215` translation shadow from the unshifted `+60 Terah` / `+130 Cainan` proof and preserve the Atonement Weave as interpretation following textual and arithmetic controls.

### 0.4 Terminology register

| Term | Meaning in `File_69` |
|---|---|
| `upper` | God or Enoch birth/head boundary in a local four-point cell. |
| `adjacent` | Adam or Enoch ascension/close boundary, one local generation after the upper boundary. |
| `+110 rail` | The earlier BC-coordinate member, exactly `110` from the no-rail member. |
| `no rail` | The base member before the local `+110` comparison is opened. |
| `resolution` | The number of times the fixed `110` translation is applied: once per field, week, or generation. |
| `corresponding boundary` | Same node-class compared across schematics or rails. |
| `crossed boundary` | Different node-classes intentionally paired, such as Lukan birth to Enochic ascension. |
| `full outer envelope` | Earliest upper `+110` boundary to latest adjacent no-rail boundary. |
| `listed-position count` | Count of occupied schematic cells or names, not automatically the interval count between two ordinary textual names. |
| `exile-bearing Abraham head bracket` | `2876/2960 BC`, generated by the completed `42`-slot state to `AD 65`. It is not Abraham's literal birth. |
| `undoubled Abraham head` | `2806/2888 BC`, generated by the `41`-figure state to `AD 65`. |
| `count-clutch` | A one-position change in genealogical attribution or occupancy. Jeconiah's overlap and second Cainan's retention/expunction are analogous count-clutches but not identical textual operations. |
| `covenant datum` | Jan 1, AD 1, the naming / circumcision hinge used by File_13. |
| `crossed Abrahamic Mirror` | Phase-resolved pairing of the forwarded doubled and undoubled Abraham states across the covenant datum. It is Mirror coordinate-completion, not ordinary civil elapsed time. |
| `slot–rail exchange` | Replacing the omitted `70/72` slot with the `+110` rail, leaving residual `40/38` and translating the full-envelope center. |
| `Enochian` | The File_68 Enochic schematic or the `364` calendar, as locally declared. Context must distinguish the two. |
| `carrier` | The ordinary same-rail generation length, `70` or `72`. |
| `generation-resolved step` | One carrier plus one exact rail: `180/182`. |
| `week-resolved step` | One complete seven-generation week plus one exact rail: `600/614`. |
| `fractal-like` | Discrete recurrence of the same ordered cell, count ratios, and node incidence under declared substitutions; not a claim of continuous geometric fractal dimension. |
| `cumulative substrate` | The Final-File_09 Creation–Cainan–Shelah–Enoch arithmetic received after File_69's inherited coordinate families have been generated. It corroborates but does not define the generator. |
| `source-controlled cumulative cell` | A File_18 row or inherited cumulative envelope. It remains distinct from a derived companion or complement output. |
| `derived companion` | A coordinate cell generated by a declared File_09 operator from source-controlled states, such as `14501–14494 BC` or `8131–8124 BC`. It is not a raw File_18 row. |
| `actual Enoch close` | `8132–8125 BC`, the cumulative Methuselah-birth / Enoch age-365-close cell. It is not Enoch's birth cell. |
| `completed Enoch rail` | `8131–8124 BC`, the one-year-forward `5880=12×490` companion. It does not correct the actual source rail. |
| `figurative-Adam complement axis` | `8622–8615 BC` or `8621–8614 BC`, one `490` above the corresponding Enoch rail. It is not a historical or cumulative Adam row. |
| `same-side complement` | An admitted same-side BC subtraction from `8132` or `8622`. It is not formal Mirror, inverse-number processing, or civil cross-axis arithmetic. |
| `coordinate echo` | A complement span whose value matches an existing schematic coordinate label while preserving distinct object and event classes. |
| `carrier-equivalent duration` | A total expressed as `n×483` or `n×490` for count comparison. It does not assign that duration to every historical generation. |
| `scalar partition` | An equality among totals, such as `5880+6720=12600`, without a claim that one component-wise coordinate route executes every term. |
| `coordinate execution` | A declared endpoint-to-endpoint operation on one named rail. It must not be inferred merely from a scalar partition. |
| `overlapping-heptad sum / union width` | The two seven-year spans total `14` as lengths, while their one-year-overlapped outer union is `13=6+1+6`. |
| `toledot` / **תּוֹלְדֹת** | A Genesis literary heading meaning generations, descendants, or account. It is a structural heading, not a dated event. |
| `toledot section` | One of the traditional ten major literary sections of Genesis. Esau's section contains formula occurrences at Genesis 36:1 and 36:9. |
| `shemot` / **שְׁמוֹת** | “Names.” Genesis 46:8 and Exodus 1:1 introduce the sons of Israel by name at the Egyptian threshold. |
| `first-toledot literary hinge` | Genesis 2:4 as the seam after the completed `6+1` frame and before the recapitulated Day-6 human-formation narrative. It is not a chronological date assignment. |
| `toledot–shemot corridor` | The bounded literary and regular-MT movement from the Genesis Creation/humanity headings through Noah, Shem, Terah, Isaac, and Jacob to Israel's named Entry. |
| `Shem–Terah seam` | The overlapping literary boundary in Genesis 11:26–27 where Terah age `70` and the three sons close Shem's genealogy and are repeated under Terah's heading. |
| `local decennial resolution` | `d=10=70÷7`, used to compare count grammar at one-seventh scale. It is not an authorized historical generation. |
| `death-state ingress` | Variant-generated Noah or Shem death labels that enter the regular-MT corridor while retaining distinct figure and operator classes. |
| `shared-label co-registration` | Two or more node classes occupying the same BC label under declared states. Co-registration may be evidential without implying event identity. |
| `unshifted regular core` | The ordinary MT Standard-Normal / `+215` position before application of the optional whole-system `−215` translation. “Unshifted” does not remove the underlying standard-position state; it excludes only the later `−215` execution from the proof-bearing corridor. |
| `translation shadow` | A uniform whole-system companion, here the quarantined `−215` field. It preserves form but is not executed inside the unshifted proof. |
| `Atonement Weave` | The theological reading of the controlled `6+1+6+1`, `13/14`, and nested recovery structures. Arithmetic and textual placement precede interpretation. |
| `Module 2 primary proof` | The Genesis-literary / regular-MT Terah-to-*shemot* corridor. It carries the new intentionality burden. |
| `Module 1 secondary corroboration` | The regular Enoch and Shem–Isaac relations. They confirm integration but do not carry the principal proof. |

### 0.5 Claim-status hierarchy

| Layer | Permitted claim |
|---|---|
| Arithmetic fact | Exact integer span, product, ratio, phase-pair result, or civil cross-axis result under a named state. |
| Source-controlled cumulative datum | File_18 row or inherited cumulative envelope used under its existing source and node class. |
| Derived / generated cumulative coordinate | Final-File_09 companion or reopened-File_69 output admitted only under its declared operator and non-promotion guard. |
| Carrier-equivalent duration | Exact `n×483/490` comparison total; not a literal equal-duration biography for every named figure. |
| Same-side complement output | Exact `8132−x` or `8622−x` scalar whose coordinate echo is interpreted only after node-class control. |
| Textual / genealogical datum | Matthew's `41` male figures in the begetting chain, its literary three fourteens, Jeconiah's deportation seam, Luke's backward genealogy, and Enoch / Jubilees textual motifs. |
| Literary-heading datum | The position and named subject of a Genesis *toledot* or *shemot* formula. A heading is not thereby assigned the date of its subject or first dated notice. |
| Regular chronology datum | A parent-controlled birth, death, wedding, call, or Entry node under the stated MT state. |
| Variant-generated regular node | A date generated by a named `+60`, `+130`, Gear, or other authorized regular operator. It must retain its operator and may not become a source-text date. |
| Local-resolution state | A controlled count comparison under `d=10=70÷7`. It is a measuring resolution, not a fourth authorized generation. |
| Literary-chronological co-registration | A heading seam, biographical datum, and regular node align under declared states. The alignment is structural evidence only after node-class non-collapse. |
| Strong structural inference | Enoch as common numerical hinge; Jeconiah as leading overlap; Matthew's `14\|13\|14` as local microcosm; nested `1/3 + 2/3`; resolution persistence; external `14+13` / internal `13+14`; covenant `6\|7\|8`; Mosaic center invariance. |
| Mechanistic inference | A coordinate family is generated by one stated count, carrier, rail, terminal, phase rule, and resolution rather than selected independently after the fact. |
| Plausibly ancient shared grammar | Heptadic, sabbatical, `360/364`, eighth-day, and genealogical slot structures were available within Second Temple chronography. |
| Theological / typological reading | Creation, Sabbath, new Creation, circumcision, curse, dynastic disqualification, reconciliation, Watchers, Exodus, Conquest, Josephic descent/exaltation, and Christological fulfillment. |
| Atonement-Weave interpretation | The reading of the controlled `6+1+6+1` and nested `13→14` structures as Fall, covering, recovery, sacrifice, reconciliation, resurrection, and ascent. It follows the textual and arithmetic proof. |
| Providential / over-intended synchronization | Exact cross-tradition alignments exceeding demonstrable conscious authorial design. |
| Excluded claim | Arithmetic or literary compatibility alone proves direct dependence, dates a *toledot* heading, makes every shared label one event, converts `10` into an authorized generation, or proves conscious knowledge of every repository coordinate. |

### 0.6 Argument-control rules

1. Arithmetic and node-class controls carry the proof.
2. The coordinate tables must be generated from the declared counts, carrier, rail, terminal, phase rule, and resolution before thematic interpretation is introduced.
3. A shared coordinate does not create identity between Enoch, Adam, Abraham, Isaac, Jeconiah, Cainan, Creation, Shem, Flood, Exodus, Joshua, or Christ.
4. Luke's `77` and `76` are both valid only under their stated endpoint pairings.
5. Matthew's undoubled inventory is `14|13|14=41`; its declared `14|14|14=42` requires the Jeconiah exile overlap. The text does not contain forty-two distinct male figures in the begetting chain.
6. The reduced `62=21+41` composite is a comparison state. The external Luke–Matthew–Enochic spacing theorem continues to use the leading `63=21+42` full-outer composite.
7. `2876/2960 BC` are exile-bearing Abraham head brackets; `2806/2888 BC` are undoubled Abraham heads. Count-state attribution changes on the fixed grid without promoting any label to a literal Abraham birth date.
8. The second Christ generation `AD 65→AD 135/137` is a privileged post-terminal state and does not supply Matthew's missing internal slot.
9. `180/182` and `600/614` are resolution states. They do not replace `70/72`.
10. `600/614` means `7g+110`; it must not be recalculated as `7(g+110)`, which yields `1260/1274`.
11. Strict `72` states and transported-primary-cell overlays must remain separate.
12. “Fractal-like” means discrete self-similarity under controlled operators. It does not authorize free scaling, coordinate selection, or a claim of continuous geometric fractal dimension.
13. The BJ overlap at `3856 BC` is strong compatibility, not proof that Jubilees encoded the complete File_69 construction.
14. Direct historical and source-critical claims remain bounded by their controlling files.
15. The Adam-to-Moses `27` is an occupied-generation-row count. It requires restored/native second Cainan and must not be confused with the `26` head-to-head intervals between twenty-seven named figures.
16. The Mosaic continuation branches at Jacob through Levi. It does not alter the Gospel genealogies' textual continuation through Judah.
17. The `26/27/28` neighboring states change named attribution or add the figurative God bookend; they do not move the fixed coordinate grid or create a divine biological chronology.
18. Jeconiah's single-versus-overlapped operation and second Cainan's expunged-versus-retained operation are analogous one-position count-clutches. They are not the same textual event, chronology, curse, or person.
19. Advancing six or eight completed years in BC chronology lowers the numeric BC labels. The `+6/+8` language is theological forwarding, not signed addition to the year number.
20. The `5670/5832` crossed fields are phase-resolved Mirror coordinate-completions. They are not ordinary same-side or civil cross-axis genealogical spans.
21. The `2876→2386 BC` Matthew-to-Moses branch is a schematic Levi-line overlay. It does not insert Moses into Matthew's textual Judah genealogy or promote `2386 BC` to a historical Moses date.
22. The primary `3366→2386→1406 BC=980+980` field is a `28`-carrier-cell coordinate envelope and must not be confused with the retained-Cainan `27` occupied-name inventory.
23. `2386 BC` and `2456 BC` retain their File_68 week-boundary and occupant functions even when File_69 gives them a generated Matthean Mosaic-close function.
24. The center labels remain node-distinct: `2386` is the inner double-`14` midpoint; `1406` is the primary outer center and historical Conquest; `1446` is the primary rail-exchanged Exodus center; `1448` is the strict `72` companion center; `1486` is the companion rail-exchanged Moses-age-40 center.
25. Slot–rail exchange is exact only under its declared paired states: `110−70=40` and `110−72=38`. It does not authorize arbitrary substitution of rail values for generations elsewhere.
26. Final File_09 controls the cumulative substrate. File_69 controls only its downstream cross-schematic interpretation and does not use the substrate to retrodefine the inherited coordinate generator.
27. The whole-year cumulative Creation cell `2|3|2`, File_63's `3|2|2` cell, and exact `3.5` Aaron phase are distinct active states.
28. Enoch birth remains `8497–8490 BC`; `8132–8125 BC` is the actual Methuselah-birth / Enoch-close cell; `8131–8124 BC` is a completed companion.
29. The figurative-Adam cells `8622–8615/8621–8614 BC` are complement axes only and are not Creation or Adam source rows.
30. Same-side complement arithmetic is not formal Mirror, inverse, or civil cross-axis execution.
31. Complement outputs are scalar spans first. Matching a File_68/File_69 or BJ coordinate creates a controlled echo, not event or occupant identity.
32. `26/27/28` are attribution counts; `26/27×483/490` are carrier-equivalent durations. Neither object class may overwrite the other.
33. Shared totals `5880`, `12600`, and `12740` require their endpoints and node classes. Equal totals do not imply equal routes.
34. `5880+6720=12600` is a scalar partition; the lower Enoch projection uses the actual `8132–8125 BC` source rail unless another execution is declared.
35. `7+483+7=497`; `7+483` and `483+7` are two overlapping `490` spans; `7+490+7=504` is a distinct completed-center field.
36. Final File_09's `14487→1446 BC=27×483` is Exodus-directed and distinct from File_63's Temple-directed `27×483` execution.
37. The complement network supports compatibility and providential synchronization only; it does not prove direct Luke, Matthew, Enoch, or Jubilees dependence on the full cumulative apparatus.
38. Genesis 2:4 follows the completed six-day work and seventh-day Sabbath and opens a recapitulated Day-6 human-formation account. The heading itself is not assigned Day 6, Day 7, Day 13, Day 14, or a BC coordinate.
39. The Genesis map uses ten major *toledot* sections, while recognizing two Esau formula occurrences. A literary heading, the biography it opens, and a chronology node attached to its subject remain distinct.
40. Genesis 10:1 is the *toledot* of Noah's sons; Genesis 11:10 is Shem's individual *toledot*. Neither may replace the other.
41. Noah's and Shem's death dates are derived from textual ages and chronology states. Do not quote the headings as if they explicitly state those death dates.
42. Genesis 5:3 gives Adam age `130` at Seth; Genesis 5:6 gives Seth age `105` at Enosh. File_69 uses `130` as the bounded ingress and records `105` without opening the secondary `235/1890/1715` branches.
43. The principal regular-MT proof uses the unshifted `2296–2091` and `2236–2031 BC` Terah biographies. The whole-system `−215` companions remain a translation shadow and may not supply nodes to the core accordion.
44. `d=10=70÷7` is a local resolution for count grammar. It is not a fourth authorized generational unit, does not replace File_15's `100/70/40`, and does not authorize division of civil coordinates.
45. Shared labels such as `2296`, `2236`, `2166`, `2091`, `2016`, and `1876 BC` may constitute controlled co-registration only after figure, event, state, node class, and operator are named.
46. `2026 BC` is Isaac's wedding at age `40` under the Isaac-*toledot* narrative. It is not the date on which Genesis 25:19 was written or “occurred,” and it is not Jacob's *toledot* node.
47. The `70` persons of Genesis 46:27 / Exodus 1:5, the Jacobean `70`-year carrier, and the `420=6×70` corridor are related but non-identical object classes.
48. Regular restored Cainan uses `+130`; cumulative restored Cainan uses `+460`; regular Cainan's lifespan may also equal `460`. The two operators and the lifespan must never be interchanged silently.
49. Module 2—the Terah-to-*shemot* regular-MT corridor—carries the principal new proof burden. Module 1—regular Enoch and Shem–Isaac—remains corroborative and primarily appendix-routed.

### 0.8 Thesis

> **Enoch-Centered Genealogical Accordion Thesis.** Final File_68 supplies the fixed mainframe: ordinary carriers `70/72`, exact rail `110`, common terminal `AD 65`, Enochic `49`, and the three accordion resolutions. File_69 adds the Lukan `77`, Matthean `63`, and Enochic `50` full-outer counts and places Enoch seven positions after each figurative God head. The master coordinate rule generates the Luke / Matthew / Enochic Enoch tables mechanically. At generation resolution, `70+110=180` and `72+110=182` reveal the paired `360/364` calendar halves and the `13/13.5/14` reconciliation envelopes. The ordinary `70/72` carriers then reproduce the same incidence without expansion. The weekly `600/614` state groups the same fixed lattice once per completed seven-generation week and produces the Watcher / Exodus–Joshua interfaces. Matthew remains the numerical center; BJ Creation `3856 BC` lands on Matthew's ordinary Enoch head; and the Lukan / Matthean heads complete the regular and cumulative MT Creation and Flood interfaces surrounding the File_68 corridor.

> **Matthean microcosm corollary.** Matthew's undoubled `41`-figure inventory is `14|13|14`. It therefore contains locally both directions that govern the larger system: its left pair is the external Luke–Matthew–Enochic `14+13=27`, while its right pair is the internal retained-Cainan Adam-to-Moses `13+14=27`. The two `27`-row fields overlap in the central `13`, so `27+27−13=41`. Jeconiah's position at and after the deportation supplies one additional occupied slot, converting the central `13` to `14` and yielding Matthew's declared `14|14|14=42`. The Matthean count discrepancy is therefore not peripheral; it is the local textual embodiment of the repository's fundamental `14/13` grammar.

> **Covenant-Mirror and Mosaic-center corollary.** The forwarded doubled and undoubled Abraham states cross the Jan 1, AD 1 covenant datum in `81` generations under both carriers: `5670=81×70=3×27×70` after six completed years, and `5832=81×72=3×27×72` after eight. Their mean is Sabbath `7`, joining old Creation `6`, rest `7`, and new Creation / circumcision `8`. Jeconiah's forwarded row reproduces `70+840=910`, linking his `41↔42` count-clutch with the Kenan/Cainan sector. The same count architecture then opens the Levi branch and the `+110` slot–rail exchange: `1406 BC` remains the primary no-rail center, `1446 BC` becomes the primary rail-exchanged center, `1448 BC` is the strict companion center, and `1486 BC` is the companion rail-exchanged center. The dates are outputs of one declared mechanism, not independent inputs selected for resemblance.

> **Cumulative-substrate and Enoch-complement corollary.** Final File_09 independently supplies a cumulative Creation–Cainan substrate compatible with File_69's `70/72`, `180/182`, `490/504`, `600/614`, and `26/27/28` states. The Shelah seam expresses the same `6/7` grammar that underlies the genealogical `13/14` architecture. The actual cumulative Enoch close `8132–8125 BC`, its completed `5880=12×490` companion, and their figurative-Adam counterparts establish two controlled same-side complement axes. Their one-year displacement preserves same-rail `7+7=14` while producing interlaced `6+7` or `7+6=13`. The finalized §5A argument executes the full complement network in §5A and Appendices A.7/B.7.

> **Genesis *Toledot–Shemot* regular-MT corollary.** The first *toledot* supplies the literary prototype after the completed `6+1` Creation frame; the later headings contract the narrative through Noah, Shem, Terah, Isaac, and Jacob toward Israel's *shemot*. The Shem–Terah seam generates the unshifted `+60 Terah` state through `205−75−70=60`. At local resolution `d=10=70÷7`, one declared `2296 BC` head produces `6/7→13/14→27/28→41/42` and the full corridor `140+140+140=420=42×10=6×70`. Isaac's age-`40` wedding supplies the double-`140` center; Cainan supplies the `2300=5×460` cumulative–regular seal; Jacob's textual `130` joins Enoch's `980` through `980+280=1260`. Module 2 carries the independent proof; Module 1 supplies secondary Enoch–Isaac corroboration. The regular chronology therefore confirms that File_69's count grammar is saturated across schematic, cumulative, and regular registers.

## 1. File_68 handoff and the Enoch-centered mechanism

### 1.1 What File_68 already controls

Final File_68 is the parent proof. It already establishes:

- the ordinary `70/72` carriers;
- the exact `110` translation rail;
- the common `AD 65` terminal;
- the Enochic `49=7×7` body and symbolic pre-generation;
- the paired `490/504` weeks and `600/614` forward diagonals;
- the generation-resolved `180/182` cells and `1260/1274` half-weeks;
- the MT-contoured Noah–Joshua corridor;
- the distinct Joshua `21`, Enochic `49`, and Lukan `76` corresponding-boundary counts.

File_69 does not re-prove those controls. It asks what happens when the same mechanism is extended through Luke and Matthew.

### 1.2 The invariant four-point cell

Let the ordinary carrier be:

```text
g = 70 or 72,
```

and let the exact rail be:

```text
T = 110.
```

If `x` is the upper no-rail boundary of a local row, the complete cell is generated mechanically:

```text
x + T       — upper, +110 rail
x           — upper, no rail
x + T − g   — adjacent, +110 rail
x − g       — adjacent, no rail.
```

The cell therefore preserves four invariants:

| Invariant | Primary carrier | Companion carrier |
|---|---:|---:|
| rail separation | `110` | `110` |
| same-rail generation | `70` | `72` |
| internal overlap `T−g` | `40` | `38` |
| full outer width `T+g` | `180` | `182` |

The local Enoch birth-to-ascension generation remains `70/72`; the outer `180/182` appears only when the two rails are crossed.

### 1.3 The master coordinate formula

The full-outer counts are:

```text
Luke = 77,
Matthew = 63,
Enochic = 50.
```

Enoch stands seven positions after each figurative God head, so the remaining counts from Enoch birth/head to `AD 65` are:

```text
70, 56, 43.
```

For either the ordinary step:

```text
u = g,
```

or the generation-resolved step:

```text
u = g + 110,
```

the no-rail coordinates follow one rule:

```text
God head = N×u − 64,
Enoch birth/head = (N−7)×u − 64.
```

The `−64` is the no-year-zero form of the `AD 65` terminal:

```text
BC + 65 − 1 = span.
```

The other three Enoch boundaries are then forced:

```text
birth +110 = birth no rail + 110,
ascension no rail = birth no rail − g,
ascension +110 = birth +110 − g.
```

Two applications of the same `110` operator must remain distinct:

1. **embedded resolution:** `u=g+110` places one `110` inside every counted generation-resolved step;
2. **boundary pairing:** the displayed `+110` column translates the already generated no-rail boundary once.

The second operation creates the paired four-point cell; it is not another generation. This one formula generates the ordinary `70/72` tables and the generation-resolved `180/182` tables. The weekly `600/614` state uses a different grouping rule and is therefore deferred until after the ordinary carrier has been established.

### 1.4 What “fractal-like” means here

The result is fractal-like in a controlled, discrete sense:

1. the same ordered four-point cell recurs;
2. Enoch remains seven positions after the figurative God head;
3. the Luke / Matthew / Enochic count differences remain `14`, `13`, and `27`;
4. Matthew remains the midpoint of the crossed `13/14` envelopes;
5. `AD 65` remains the common ordinary terminal;
6. the substitution `70→72`, `180→182`, or `600→614` changes measures without changing incidence.

This is not a claim of continuous geometric self-similarity, a fractal dimension, or free numerical scaling. It is a finite operator-recursive lattice.

### 1.5 Luke moves backward; Matthew moves forward

Luke 3 moves genealogically backward from Jesus toward Adam and God. Matthew begins with Abraham and moves forward toward Jesus. Under File_69's extended pre-Abraham state, the two directions meet at Enoch:

```text
Luke:      Christ → Adam/God, traced backward
Matthew:   Abraham/Creation → Christ, traced forward
Common hinge: Enoch
```

Creation supplies the outer bookends, but Enoch supplies the operating midpoint.

### 1.6 The calendrical hinge at Wednesday morning

`File_12` controls the Enochian solar vector at Wednesday morning at sunrise, tied to the creation of the luminaries. This point bisects the Creation week:

```text
3.5 days + 3.5 days = 7 days.
```

Under the day-year / calendar-scaling principle:

```text
3.5 × 360 = 1260,
3.5 × 364 = 1274.
```

The same midpoint appears genealogically. Luke descends `1260` toward Enoch, and Matthew continues `1260` away from Enoch under the primary full accordion. The calendar begins at the middle of Creation week, and Enoch's generation occupies the middle of the Luke–Matthew Creation field.

| Enochic | `50=1+49` | `50×70=3500` | symbolic pre-generation plus seven weeks |

Matthew also has a valid reduced comparison state:

That `62` records the pre-Abraham `21` plus Matthew's undoubled named-figure inventory. It does not replace the leading `63` in the external theorem, because the outer Luke–Matthew–Enochic field uses Matthew's declared and Jeconiah-completed `42`-slot execution.

### 1.9 Matthew's `14|13|14` base and `14|14|14` completion

Matthew 1:2–16 contains `41` male genealogical figures in the begetting chain from Abraham through Jesus. Strictly, forty are antecedent figures and Jesus is the terminal figure; “forty-one named genealogical figures” is therefore more precise than “forty-one forefathers.” The women named in the narrative remain textually important but are not separate begetting-chain positions in this count.

Without repeating a boundary figure, the `41` positions can be partitioned in three ways:

| Undoubled partition | First block | Second block | Third block |
|---|---|---|---|
| `14+14+13` | Abraham–David | Solomon–Jeconiah | Shealtiel–Jesus |
| **`14+13+14`** | **Abraham–David** | **Solomon–Josiah** | **Jeconiah–Jesus** |
| `13+14+14` | Abraham–Jesse | David–Josiah | Jeconiah–Jesus |

The structurally preferred undoubled form is:

```text
Abraham → David
= 14 figures,

Solomon → Josiah
= 13 figures,

Jeconiah → Jesus
= 14 figures.
```

Therefore:

```text
14 | 13 | 14
= 41.
```

This form preserves both Matthean outer fourteens while placing Jeconiah at the opening of the final block. It also contains two overlapping `27`-row fields:

```text
14 + 13
= 27,
```

```text
13 + 14
= 27,
```

so:

```text
27 + 27 − 13
= 41.
```

Matthew's own name inventory therefore contains locally both directional forms that govern the larger File_69 system.

Jeconiah is the natural one-slot completion. Matthew 1:11 places him at the deportation, while Matthew 1:12 resumes after the deportation with Jeconiah begetting Shealtiel. Jeconiah can therefore occupy both sides of the seam:

```text
14 + (13 + 1 exile overlap) + 14
= 14 + 14 + 14
= 42 occupied slots.
```

The two Matthean ledgers are simultaneous:

| State | Count | Primary `70` Abraham coordinate | Companion `72` Abraham coordinate |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Undoubled named-figure state | `14+13+14=41` | `2806 BC` | `2888 BC` |
| Jeconiah-overlapped slot state | `14+14+14=42` | `2876 BC` | `2960 BC` |

The arithmetic is exact:

```text

= 2940
= 42×70,
```

```text
2806 BC → AD 65
= 2870
= 41×70,
```

```text
2876 − 2806
= 70.
```

The companion reproduces the same one-generation difference:

```text
2960 BC → AD 65
= 3024
= 42×72,
```

```text
2888 BC → AD 65
= 2952
= 41×72,
```

```text
2960 − 2888
= 72.
```

The exact terminology is therefore:

- `2876/2960 BC` — **Matthean exile-bearing Abraham head bracket**;
- `2806/2888 BC` — **Matthean undoubled Abraham head**.

Neither is Abraham's literal birth date. On the fixed grid, the Jeconiah clutch changes occupied-row attribution by one carrier without changing the governing terminal.

Christ's second generation remains a separate privileged state:

```text
AD 65 → AD 135/137.
```

It does not supply Matthew's missing internal slot. Jeconiah solves the internal count; Christ supplies the post-terminal double generation.

## 3. The ordinary `70/72` carriers and the Matthew–Jubilees hinge

### 3.1 Return to the ordinary carriers

The generation-resolved system is not the base chronology. It is a declared expansion of the fixed File_68 carriers.

At ordinary resolution:

### 3.4B Matthew's `14|13|14` count-clutch and the Jeconiah–Cainan seam

Matthew's undoubled inventory is:

- the God row is figurative and supplies no biological chronology.

The theological analogy is bounded but meaningful. Jeremiah 22:30 says:

> `כִּתְבוּ אֶת־הָאִישׁ הַזֶּה עֲרִירִי ... כִּי לֹא יִצְלַח מִזַּרְעוֹ אִישׁ יֹשֵׁב עַל־כִּסֵּא דָוִד`
>

Jeconiah is not biologically childless; his descendants continue in 1 Chronicles 3 and Matthew 1. The judgment is dynastic: he remains genealogically present while being disqualified from successful Davidic enthronement. File_53's second-Cainan state likewise preserves historical memory while expunging the figure from the purified sacred count. The common structural principle is:

```text
historical retention
+
covenantal or dynastic disqualification
≠
erasure from history.
```

The restoration patterns also correspond without becoming identical:

```text

expunged / corrupted position
→ purified successor position,
```

```text
Jeconiah → Zerubbabel:
removed signet
→ restored signet language in Haggai 2:23.
```

Zerubbabel remains governor, not an ordinary restored king, so Jeremiah's dynastic judgment is not cancelled.

> **Matthean `14|13|14` Microcosm Theorem.** Matthew's `41`-figure undoubled inventory is `14|13|14`. Its first two blocks reproduce the external `14+13=27` Luke–Matthew–Enochic direction; its last two reproduce the internal `13+14=27` retained-Cainan Adam-to-Moses direction. The two `27`-row fields overlap in the central `13`, giving `27+27−13=41`. Jeconiah's deportation overlap adds one occupied slot to that central block, producing Matthew's declared `14|14|14=42`. After six completed years of Creation forwarding, Jeconiah's row reproduces `70+840=910`, so Jeconiah functions numerically as a new first Kenan and compositionally as a second-Cainan-like count clutch. The result strengthens the shared chronographic grammar and providential reading; it does not identify Jeconiah with Cainan, erase their textual differences, or prove conscious Matthean execution of the full File_69 system.

## 5B. The Genesis *Toledot–Shemot* Atonement Corridor — The Regular-MT One-Seventh Accordion

Proof-burden order:

1. **Module 2 — principal independent proof:** the Genesis literary spine and the unshifted regular-MT chronology supply the textual frame and arithmetic proof;
2. the Cainan rectangles join regular and cumulative chronology;
3. **Module 1 — secondary corroboration:** the Enoch–Isaac module supplies the cross-scale confirmation;
4. the Atonement Weave supplies the theological interpretation after the textual and arithmetic layers.

The principal result is not generated from the File_69 Enoch-centered coordinate formula. It is recovered independently from the ordinary patriarchal chronology under declared `+60 Terah`, regular restored-Cainan `+130`, and base-state controls. That independence is why §5B can test whether the `6/7`, `13/14`, `27/28`, and `41/42` structures already established in the schematic and cumulative systems are isolated or saturated throughout the repository.

### 5B.1 The first *toledot* — completed `6+1` and the reopened Day 6

Genesis 2:1–3 closes the six-day work and the seventh-day Sabbath. Genesis 2:4 then introduces the first **תּוֹלְדֹת** heading:

```text
אֵלֶּה תוֹלְדוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ בְּהִבָּרְאָם
“These are the generations of the heavens and the earth when they were created.”
```

The narrative opened by that heading recapitulates the formation of the man. The first *toledot* therefore stands at the literary seam:

```text
Genesis 1:1–2:3:
completed six-day work + seventh-day rest
= completed `6+1`

Genesis 2:4ff:
Day-6 human formation reopened
→ man
→ woman
→ Fall
→ covering and recovery.
```

The textual datum is the completed `6+1` followed by a recapitulated Day-6 account. Under that stated ceiling, the first heading supplies the literary prototype of:

A year head, a completed boundary, a Gear coordinate, the literary heading, and the later Day-13/Day-14 interpretation must not be collapsed.

### 5B.2 The ten-section *toledot* contraction and the *shemot* threshold

Under the traditional ten-major-section division, Genesis uses ten principal *toledot* sections, while Esau's major section contains formula occurrences at Genesis 36:1 and 36:9. The sequence progressively contracts the narrative from Creation to the named family of Israel.

| Major section | Reference | Immediate literary or chronological interface used here |
|---|---|---|
| Heavens and earth | Genesis 2:4 | follows the completed `6+1` and reopens the Day-6 human-formation narrative |
| Adam | Genesis 5:1 | follows Seth, Enosh, and the beginning of calling on the name of YHWH; introduces Adam's `130` |
| Noah | Genesis 6:9 | narrows the account to preserved humanity and Noah's three-son household |
| Sons of Noah | Genesis 10:1 | follows Noah's `350` post-Flood years and death; opens Shem, Ham, and Japheth in the Table of Nations |
| Shem | Genesis 11:10 | begins with Shem at `100` and carries the selected line to Terah |
| Terah | Genesis 11:27 | repeats Abram, Nahor, and Haran after Terah's age-`70` threshold and introduces Sarai |
| Ishmael | Genesis 25:12 | follows Abraham's death and disposes the collateral line |
| Isaac | Genesis 25:19 | resumes the covenant line and immediately gives Isaac age `40` at marriage |
| Esau | Genesis 36:1, 9 | doubled collateral formula after Isaac's death |
| Jacob | Genesis 37:2 | final Genesis *toledot*, carried through Joseph's descent, exaltation, reconciliation, and preservation |

The terminal threshold changes the literary formula:

```text
Genesis 46:8
וְאֵלֶּה שְׁמוֹת בְּנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵל הַבָּאִים מִצְרַיְמָה
“And these are the names of the sons of Israel who came into Egypt.”
```

Genesis 46:27 and Exodus 1:5 state the MT total as `70`. The movement is therefore:

```text
תּוֹלְדֹת — generations / family account
→ Noah
→ Shem
→ Terah
→ Isaac
→ Jacob through Joseph
→ שְׁמוֹת — the named and numbered sons of Israel at Entry.
```

The `70` persons, the authorized Jacobean `70`-year carrier, and the regular corridor `420=6×70` are related but node-distinct.

The Adam heading follows the births of Seth and Enosh and the statement that people began to call on the name of YHWH. Genesis 5 then gives:

```text

```

That literary adjacency gives Noah's death-state field a legitimate ingress function without converting the heading into a date. The Gear-1 regular Noah labels generated by the `+60 Terah` and restored-Cainan `+130` operators are:

```text
  60      70      60
```

The corresponding Gear-1 Shem-death labels are:

```text
  60      70      60.
```

Every corresponding Noah/Shem state remains `150` years apart:

```text

2236−2086 = 150,

```

The four labels in each row form a controlled Cartesian variant field:

```text

= {0, 60, 130, 190}.
```

The middle `70` is the difference between the two authorized variant magnitudes:

```text

```

Most importantly, the Noah overlay already supplies the unresolved decennial field:

```text
= 60 + 70
```

The Terah household will resolve that same outer `130` and Sarah will complete it to `140`.

### 5B.4 The Shem–Terah seam and the textual generation of `+60`

Genesis 11:10 begins Shem's individual *toledot*. The genealogy runs through Arpachshad, Shelah, Eber, Peleg, Reu, Serug, Nahor, and Terah. Genesis 11:26 closes it with Terah at age `70` and the three sons Abram, Nahor, and Haran. Genesis 11:27 begins Terah's *toledot* by repeating those sons, and Genesis 11:29 introduces Sarai within the opening movement.

The boundary is therefore an overlapping literary seam:

```text
Shem's תּוֹלְדֹת
→ Terah age 70 and the three sons
→ Terah's תּוֹלְדֹת repeats the household
→ Sarai enters the narrative.
```

The `+60 Terah` state is generated by the textual ages surrounding that seam. Genesis 11:32 supplies Terah's `205`; Genesis 12:4 supplies Abram's age `75`; and Acts 7:4 supplies the death-before-departure warrant used by the harmonized death-at-call state. Under that declared reading:

```text
Terah lifespan − Abram age at call
= 205 − 75
= 130.
```

Terah is therefore `130` when Abram is born. Compared with the age-`70` firstborn threshold:

```text
130 − 70 = 60,
205 − 75 − 70 = 60.
```

The two unshifted Terah biographies are consequently:

```text
2296→2091 BC = 205,
2236→2031 BC = 205,
```

with corresponding endpoints displaced by `60`:

```text
2296−2236 = 60,
2091−2031 = 60.
```

The proof-bearing chronology uses only these unshifted states. Here “unshifted” means the ordinary MT Standard-Normal / `+215` position before applying the optional whole-system `−215` translation; it does not remove the underlying standard-position state. The two whole-system `−215` Terah biographies remain mathematically valid Appendix A.8 translation shadows, but they remain quarantined and are not a source of nodes for the principal accordion. Their exact shifted coordinates are not executed in §5B.

The Shem-to-Abram span also supplies the Module-1 handoff:

```text

```

The Terah/Abram end of that `390` contains the local `130` field:

```text
2296−2166 = 130.
```

The literary seam therefore generates the same `60/70/130` components that appear in the chronology rather than merely standing near them.

## 6. How Luke and Matthew complete the File_68 mainframe

This section returns explicitly to the parent file. File_68 supplies the source-critical seven-week core and MT-contoured corridor; File_69 supplies the Lukan and Matthean outer interfaces. The August 18 reopening adds a further conclusion: Matthew's own local count architecture is not merely compatible with the mainframe but is a compact textual model of its governing `14/13` grammar.

### 6.1 File_68's primary historical corridor

File_68's Enochic schematic most clearly parallels MT from the Flood through:

- Noah / Shem / Arphaxad;
- Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Levi, Kohath, and Amram;
- Moses, Aaron, Joshua I, and Joshua II;
- the Conquest and land-rest field.

Its downstream Temple, destruction, restoration, and Christological alignments are real but less uniformly historical.

### 6.2 The Lukan completion

Luke supplies the outer cumulative and regular interfaces:

- the `215`-translated cumulative Creation square;
- the `9890=23×430` regular / cumulative bridge;
- the `10000` Watcher-binding rail;
- the `8400=25×336` to cumulative Shem;
- the `9000=25×360` to cumulative Flood;
- the `11270=23×490` Flood relation;
- the `12500` Conquest / cumulative-Joshua envelope.

Luke therefore extends the Enochic mainframe backward into the cumulative outer chronology.

### 6.3 The Matthean completion

Matthew supplies the forward, central, and local-textual completion:

- the `2520` offset from Luke;
- the `5880=12×490` to cumulative Shem;
- the `6480=18×360` to cumulative Flood;
- the `7200=20×360` to the MT Year-6 anchor;
- the `2625=1290+1335` cumulative-Creation relation;
- the BJ `3856 BC` Enoch-head overlap;
- the undoubled `14|13|14=41` name architecture;
- the Jeconiah-completed `14|14|14=42` slot architecture;
- the local double-`27` recurrence `(14+13)` and `(13+14)`;
- the Jeconiah `70+840=910` Kenan checksum and `41↔42` Cainan-like count-clutch;
- the covenant-Mirror `5670/5832 = 3×27×70/72` fields;
- the Abraham-bracket Levi branch `2876→2386 BC=490` and the double-`14` lock `3366→2386→1406 BC=980+980`;
- the slot–rail center translations `1406→1446 BC` and `1448→1486 BC`;
- the exact midpoint role between Luke and the Enochic schematic.

Matthew therefore joins the Enochic mainframe to Man, Sabbath, old and new Creation, exile, dynastic judgment, Moses, reconciliation, and Messiah.

### 6.4 The complete regular / cumulative accounting

The resulting hierarchy is:

| Layer | Primary File_69 interface |
|---|---|
| Cumulative Creation | Lukan `215` translation and `9890` square |
| Regular Creation | standard / minimum MT square; Matthew `7200` Year-6 relation |
| Cumulative Shem | Lukan `8400`; Matthean `5880` |

Thus both the regular and cumulative MT mainframes are accounted for without forcing every coordinate into one chronology.

### 6.5 Why Enoch remains the key

The systems do not interlock because all of them share one Creation date. They interlock because:

1. Enoch is seventh from the figurative God bookend;
2. the Enochian solar calendar begins at the middle of Creation week;
3. Luke and Matthew spread in opposite directions around Enoch;
4. the external `14+13` and internal `13+14` directions meet inside Matthew's `14|13|14`;
5. BJ Creation lands on Matthew's Enoch head;
6. the Watcher `460/2300/4600` field translates Enoch into Exodus and Joshua;

### 7.5 Historical and authorial claim ceiling

The strongest justified claim is:

> Luke, Matthew, 1 Enoch, Jubilees, and the Pentateuchal genealogical traditions are mathematically compatible with a shared Second Temple heptadic and calendrical chronographic grammar. Matthew's own `14|13|14` count is the local textual microcosm of the larger `14+13 / 13+14` architecture, while the covenant Mirror and Mosaic center matrix complete that grammar in calendrical and chronological form. The combined structure is consistent with providential over-intention beyond demonstrable conscious authorial design.

The prior existence of the internal `13+14` Pentateuchal pattern materially reduces the risk that the external `14+13` structure was imposed only after the fact. Matthew's local `14|13|14` further reduces that risk because it contains both directions before any outer coordinate is applied. The independent double-`14` Mosaic lock, Jeconiah `910/840` checksum, `81=3×27` Mirror, and slot–rail center exchange provide separate corroboration. The new regular-MT axis provides a still more independent control because its nodes arise from Genesis ages, births, deaths, marriage, and Entry rather than from the File_69 Enoch-centered coordinate formula.

The present file does not prove:

- direct Matthean dependence on the complete Book of Enoch chronology;
- direct Lukan dependence on Jubilees;
- that Jeconiah and second Cainan are one textual device;
- conscious authorial knowledge of every generated repository coordinate;
- that generated schematic dates are literal historical dates for Enoch, Adam, Abraham, Jeconiah, or Creation;
- that proleptic calendar conversion itself creates the theological meaning.

It also does not claim that every patriarch literally lived or generated for `483/490` years.

### 7.7 Regular-MT Atonement-Weave claim ceiling

The new module establishes four ordered layers.

#### Textual and literary layer

Genesis places the first *toledot* after the completed six-day work and seventh-day Sabbath and then reopens the Day-6 human narrative. The later headings contract the account through Adam, Noah, the sons of Noah, Shem, Terah, Isaac, Esau, and Jacob. Jacob's final *toledot* is carried through Joseph to the *shemot* of Israel entering Egypt. These are textual and literary data; the headings themselves are not assigned the dates of their subjects.

#### Arithmetic layer

Under the unshifted regular-MT states, one declared `2296 BC` head generates exactly:

at the local one-seventh resolution `d=10=70÷7`, and:

The ordinary regular chronology independently reproduces the same ordered `6/7`, `13/14`, `27/28`, and `41/42` grammar already present in Matthew, the Luke–Matthew–Enochic schematic, the retained/omitted Cainan count states, and the cumulative Enoch phase clutch. This cross-register saturation materially reduces the likelihood that those structures are isolated products of one later coordinate construction.

The strongest justified design claim is:

> The source-controlled regular, cumulative, and schematic systems are arranged in a highly integrated common grammar. The ordered recurrence through independent literary headings, biographical ages, variant mechanisms, and cross-modal rectangles strongly supports deliberate compositional design or providential over-intention.

It does not prove that one human author consciously possessed every repository coordinate or that Luke, Matthew, Genesis, Jubilees, and 1 Enoch directly borrowed the complete system from one another.

#### Theological layer

The repository interprets the common grammar as the Atonement Weave:

```text
6 + 1 + 6 + 1
→ human creation and Sabbath
→ Fall
→ covering and recovery
→ sacrifice and reconciliation
→ death, resurrection, and ascent.
```

The exact Day-13/Day-14 placement and garments-of-skins/Cross correspondence remain theological and typological interpretation. They follow the textual and arithmetic lattice and may not replace it.

## 8. Conclusion

File_68 established the Enochic seven-week mainframe, the exact `110` translation, the common `AD 65` terminal, the dual `70/72` carriers, the three accordion resolutions, and the Flood-to-Joshua MT-contoured corridor.

File_69 shows why Luke and Matthew belong above and around that mainframe, and why Matthew's count discrepancy belongs at the center of the argument rather than at its margin.

The proof proceeds in the order demanded by the mechanism:

1. **generation-resolved `180/182`** applies the `110` rail once per generation, revealing the complete rail cell, the `360/364` calendar halves, and the `13/13.5/14` reconciliation envelopes.
2. **ordinary `70/72`** confirms the same Luke–Matthew–Enochic incidence without full accordion expansion, places Matthew's Enoch head at BJ Creation `3856 BC`, and reveals the external `14+13` / internal `13+14` reversal.
3. **Matthew's local `14|13|14`** contains both directions as overlapping `27`-row fields; Jeconiah's overlap completes the middle block and yields the declared `14|14|14`.
4. **the covenant Mirror** advances the primary state six completed years and the companion eight, centers them on Sabbath `7`, and produces `5670/5832 = 3×27×70/72`.
5. **the Abraham–Levi–Moses continuation** opens the final heptad and the sliding double-`14` field.
6. **the `+110` slot–rail exchange** preserves or translates the exact Mosaic centers `1406/1446/1448/1486 BC`.
7. **`600/614` per completed week** groups the same fixed lattice at the least obvious resolution and opens the `6000`, `2330`, `4600`, and `2300±30` Watcher–Joshua fields.
8. **the Genesis *toledot–shemot* regular-MT test** independently contracts the same count grammar to `d=10` through an explicitly interleaved cross-state coordinate field, generating `6/7→13/14→27/28→41/42`, the Isaac double-`140`, and the Terah-to-Entry `420` corridor.
9. **the Cainan and Jacob seals** join regular and cumulative chronology through `2300=5×460`, bilateral `130`, and `980+280=1260`.

Luke supplies the backward outer sweep. Matthew supplies the forward sweep, the local `14|13|14` microcosm, the Jeconiah count-clutch, and the central Man–Sabbath hinge. Genesis supplies the first-*toledot* prototype and the independent Terah-to-*shemot* decennial accordion. Jubilees places its Creation at Matthew's Enoch head and supplies the Cainan-expunction comparison. The cumulative chronology supplies the Enoch phase clutch; Cainan joins cumulative and regular chronology; Moses supplies the historical center corridor; and Christ supplies the common terminal and eighth-day completion.

The decisive architecture is one finite mechanism:

```text
fixed God / Enoch counts
→ common `AD 65`
→ exact `110` rail
→ `180/182`
→ `70/72`
→ external `14+13` / internal `13+14`
```

Matthew's `14|13|14` is the local textual microcosm. The first *toledot* is the literary prototype. The Terah-to-*shemot* accordion is the independent regular-MT proof. Cainan is the cross-modal clutch. The covenant datum is the calendrical disclosure. Moses is the historical center interface. The whole system is therefore **Enoch-centered, Matthean-microcosmic, Genesis-literary, cumulative-and-regular integrated, Creation-bounded, MT-contoured, mechanically generated, fractal-like in finite form, Atonement-patterned, and Christ-terminated**.
